Upcoming strategy bets

Every signal a strategy currently wants to act on, including the ones it is blocked on. A status of PRICE TOO HIGH means the model likes the side but the offer is not good enough — the strategy waits instead of chasing. Filters: strategy, market, team, player, goalie, season, month, metrics (combine with AND).
